# Canonical numbers (validation round, r = 30, cell C unless stated)

Generated by `scripts/sim/canonical_numbers.py`; the manuscript quotes numbers only from here, with this rounding. Regenerate after any table change; the text audit compares against this file.

## 4. Lemma 1 / Proposition 1 instantiation at r = 30

4a. Simulator-consistent (the values the text and T5 quote): sigma_2 = 0.57 measured on the simulated streams, two bursts per bell block (one per 45-min period, 50 per week), blind time ceil(d) + 1 at the executed lead: the engine runs a lead of ceil(d) one-minute slots (engine.make_context) and reveals a slot after it ends, so the 72B replica tier (d = 1.5 min, 2 slots) is blind for 3 min. The 72B row at d = 1 (blind 2 min) is the bound at the lead-time sweep's first point; the 72B runs of section 18 start at d = 1.5, which executes as 2 slots like d = 2.

| case | setting | lead_min | executed_slots | sigma2 | ell_min | blind_min | m_min | q_max_usd_wk | lemma1_lower_usd_wk | lower_over_qmax | upper_over_qmax | source |

## 6. Measured capacity ladder (batch B4, one GPU, 211-token output)

K is the largest concurrency whose measured TTFT p95 stays within the target and mu = 60 K / (TTFT at K + 211 / per-stream decode rate at K) is one replica's fluid capacity, both through the loader the sweeps use: the five cases of section 5 are the rag_shared_4000 rows at 2 s and the rag_unique_2000 / rag_unique_4000 rows at 5 s. Rows marked unreachable miss the target already at concurrency 1, so no replica count attains it.

## 7. Timing cells A-F on the laptop platform (arm64), GPU-hours per week / in-class SLO (r = 30, five seeds 10-14, six weeks, baselines tuned on their own tier)

Cells: A sync; B independent dispersion, planner at the bell; C independent, planner knows the distribution; D common shock, planner at the bell; E common, planner knows; F mixed, planner knows (parameters in section 5).

From results/runs/cells_laptop.csv (scripts/sim/runs_cells_table.py; `run` is the offset.csv under pilot/). Per case and tier the rows come from four laptop runs of one stream group, each with cells A-F: the 13-policy run and the learned-prior run of the cells-laptop queue (outputs/validation_local/runs/cells_laptop, the cloud's six-cell commands run on the laptop), the R1 TAP job (tap, offline) and section 3's six-week rerun of the resource-rule floor rules (runs/tuning_resource/<case>_<tier>/eval). Every job finished (DONE marker, exit 0), the other three passed their checks against the R1 job (invariant.json: verified), the offline rows of the four runs are equal in every column, and a policy two runs hold has the same rows in both; it is read from the first of R1 job, 13-policy run, learned-prior run and rerun. Rows named as in section 27a: cost-tuned (own tier) = the cost tuning of the tier's own slice (the job's --tuned); floor-tuned (dispersed) = the resource rule's SLO-floor configuration (section 28b). Whole run, means over the five seeds: GPU-hours per week to 1 decimal / in-class attainment to 3. Cell C holds section 27a's rows of the same name (checked, 68 whole-run and 408 weekly rows, every column, unrounded); section 32 holds every row of every cell (checked, 408 rows: GPU-hours per week and attainment, unrounded and as printed). The cloud's six-cell runs (results/sim/validation/tables/cells_long.csv) draw other streams and are not listed; section 10 reads the cloud's boundary cases from that file.

## 14. Admission-priority and penalty-grading ablation (cell C, instance tier, r = 30, five seeds; cell C re-drawn alone, so streams differ from the main run within the seed spread)

Laptop runs (arm64), seeds 10-14; the source column names the run of each row. offline and the calendar planners come from the original run (outputs/validation_local/sensitivity_sweeps or priority_ablation); reactive and velocity are the SLO-floor rules at the configurations the resource rule selected (section 28b), re-run with the original's arguments on its streams (outputs/validation_local/runs/s3_consistency/<run>; a rule whose configuration the resource rule kept reproduces the original's rows); tap comes from the TAP job of the same stream group (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/<job>, results/runs/JOBS_README.md). A run is used only if both jobs' checks passed against the original (invariant.json), the TAP job ran with the original's learned-timing prior and the offline rows of the three are equal in every column; otherwise this file is not written. TAP jobs: r1_<case>_inst (cell C of its cells A-F) for the priority-on arm, whose streams are section 27a's at the instance tier; r8_<case>_off and r8_<case>_ungraded for the other two. gpu_h_wk_vs_main_pct: GPU-hours per week against the same policy in the main setting, from unrounded means.

TAP's causality check (scripts/audit/tap_prefix_invariance.py; the arguments of the R1 instance-tier job: cell C, d = 8, r = 30): for every seed and week boundary w, TAP is re-run with every forecast ending at w, and the minutes of weeks 1..w where its committed decision or its advice sequence differs from the full run's are counted (tap_minutes_differing, advice_minutes_differing: sums over seeds and boundaries); advice_alone_equals_advice_in_tap: for every seed the advice run on its own gives the sequence it gives inside TAP; n_replans: size re-solves in the full run. ## 18. Lead-time curve (sensitivity round): cell C, r = 30, five seeds, feedback rules at the resource-rule SLO-floor configuration of the nearest measured tier (replica tuning for d <= 2 min, instance tuning above)

Laptop runs (arm64), seeds 10-14; the source column names the run of each row. offline and the calendar planners come from the original run (outputs/validation_local/sensitivity_sweeps or priority_ablation); reactive and velocity are the SLO-floor rules at the configurations the resource rule selected (section 28b), re-run with the original's arguments on its streams (outputs/validation_local/runs/s3_consistency/<run>; a rule whose configuration the resource rule kept reproduces the original's rows); tap comes from the TAP job of the same stream group (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/<job>, results/runs/JOBS_README.md). A run is used only if both jobs' checks passed against the original (invariant.json), the TAP job ran with the original's learned-timing prior and the offline rows of the three are equal in every column; otherwise this file is not written. TAP jobs: r1_<case>_rep (cell C) at d = 1 (32B) and 1.5 (72B), r1_<case>_inst (cell C) at d = 8, r3_<case>_d<d> otherwise. At d = 1, 1.5 and 8 every row is section 27a's row of the same name and tier (checked, 24 rows: GPU-hours per week and attainment, unrounded).

## 19. Timing cells G-L (sensitivity round): instance tier, r = 30, five seeds; G-J keep the total timing spread of cell C (6.64 min) and vary the share of its variance that is common to a bell

Laptop runs (arm64), seeds 10-14; the source column names the run of each row. offline and the calendar planners come from the original run (outputs/validation_local/sensitivity_sweeps or priority_ablation); reactive and velocity are the SLO-floor rules at the configurations the resource rule selected (section 28b), re-run with the original's arguments on its streams (outputs/validation_local/runs/s3_consistency/<run>; a rule whose configuration the resource rule kept reproduces the original's rows); tap comes from the TAP job of the same stream group (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/<job>, results/runs/JOBS_README.md). A run is used only if both jobs' checks passed against the original (invariant.json), the TAP job ran with the original's learned-timing prior and the offline rows of the three are equal in every column; otherwise this file is not written. TAP job: r4_<case>_cellsGL.

## 20. Reservation tier (8 min lead, hourly commit and billing blocks), cell C, r = 30, five seeds

Laptop runs (arm64), seeds 10-14; the source column names the run of each row. offline and the calendar planners come from the original run (outputs/validation_local/sensitivity_sweeps or priority_ablation); reactive and velocity are the SLO-floor rules at the configurations the resource rule selected (section 28b), re-run with the original's arguments on its streams (outputs/validation_local/runs/s3_consistency/<run>; a rule whose configuration the resource rule kept reproduces the original's rows); tap comes from the TAP job of the same stream group (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/<job>, results/runs/JOBS_README.md). A run is used only if both jobs' checks passed against the original (invariant.json), the TAP job ran with the original's learned-timing prior and the offline rows of the three are equal in every column; otherwise this file is not written. ## 22. Held-out semester (2025-26 sem. 2) with the SLO-floor variants, instance tier, r = 30, five seeds; prior from the other semester (symmetric) or the earlier one (forward)

Laptop runs (arm64), seeds 10-14; the source column names the run of each row. offline and the calendar planners come from the original run (outputs/validation_local/sensitivity_sweeps or priority_ablation); reactive and velocity are the SLO-floor rules at the configurations the resource rule selected (section 28b), re-run with the original's arguments on its streams (outputs/validation_local/runs/s3_consistency/<run>; a rule whose configuration the resource rule kept reproduces the original's rows); tap comes from the TAP job of the same stream group (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/<job>, results/runs/JOBS_README.md). A run is used only if both jobs' checks passed against the original (invariant.json), the TAP job ran with the original's learned-timing prior and the offline rows of the three are equal in every column; otherwise this file is not written. TAP jobs: r2_<case>_inst_sym (cell C of its cells A-F) and r2_<case>_inst_fwd, each with the prior of its rows (the offline rows do not depend on the prior, so the prior is checked on the jobs' command lines).

## 27. Central cell C on the laptop platform (arm64): TAP and every baseline variant on the same streams, r = 30, five seeds

From results/runs/paired_cell_c.csv (laptop runs only; every source's offline rows equal the TAP job's; `run` is the offset.csv under pilot/). The cloud rows of sections 1-3 are kept but are other streams (x86 and arm64 draw different arrivals from the same arguments). GPU-hours and starts per week to 1 decimal; USD per week is the resource cost (running + starts, without the unserved-request penalty that section 1's usd_wk includes) to 0 decimals; in-class attainment is the mean over seeds to 3 decimals, and to 4 where it lies within 0.002 of 0.95; lowest seed to 3 decimals. Tunings: cost-tuned (own tier) = tuned for cost on the tier's own slice (at the replica tier, the replica-tier tuning); cost-tuned (replica-tier tuning) = the replica tier's configuration run at the instance tier; floor-tuned (dispersed) = the SLO-floor variant of the dispersed slice selected by resource cost on the laptop's tuning week (among grid points whose mean in-class attainment over the tuning seeds is >= 0.95 unrounded, the lowest mean running + start cost; section 28b); floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection) = the configuration the penalty-inclusive cost ratio selected on the cloud's tuning week, listed where the resource rule chose another; floor-tuned (synchronous) = the SLO-floor variant of the synchronous slice (grid points reaching 0.95 on the tuning slice). The note column names the run each row comes from.

29b. Realised over forecast in-class request mass of the stream TAP plans on: sum of lam_in over sum of lam_hat_learn_prior_in, seed 10, cell C, six weeks and each week (3 decimals); the forecast books the whole roster, the stream draws attendance and cancellations (section 5, workload).

ratio_whole_run_2dp: the six-week ratio to 2 decimals.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c2 32B shared-4k | 10 | C | 4,343,098 | 5,371,737 | 0.809 | 0.81 | 0.810 | 0.814 | 0.809 | 0.804 | 0.810 | 0.806 | stream of scripts/audit/prefix_invariance.py (sweep_args, CELL_C): lam_in / lam_hat_learn_prior_in, prior data/timetable/slots_2025-2026-2.csv |
| c3 72B shared-4k | 10 | C | 4,343,098 | 5,371,737 | 0.809 | 0.81 | 0.810 | 0.814 | 0.809 | 0.804 | 0.810 | 0.806 | stream of scripts/audit/prefix_invariance.py (sweep_args, CELL_C): lam_in / lam_hat_learn_prior_in, prior data/timetable/slots_2025-2026-2.csv |

29c. When TAP's online size correction re-solves (R1 runs, cell C, seeds 10-14): the correction's rule (src/pilot/sim/policies/calendar_adapt.py: WINDOW_MIN trailing window, a check once REPLAN_EVERY_MIN minutes have passed since the last re-solve, a re-solve when the ratio moved by more than REPLAN_TOL, clip RATIO_LO / RATIO_HI, prior mass = the first teaching day's forecast mass), through the ratio() of TAP's advice (LearnedPriorAdaptPolicy, forecast lam_hat_learn_prior_in), replayed on each run's stream rebuilt from prefix_invariance.sweep_args and CELL_C at the run's tier and lead. The ratio reads only realised in-class arrivals and the forecast, so the replay solves no plan; it reproduces n_replans and final_ratio of every seed's row of the run's diagnostics.csv (section 29a). Minutes of the run count from Monday 00:00 of teaching week 1; the minute is the one the re-solve is decided at, and the rescaled plan applies from slot minute + d.

## 31. Billing sensitivity (s4 of the rerun round), laptop cell C, r = 30, five seeds (10-14): a replica start billed from the order against the main caliber

From the offset.csv in each row's source column, through scripts/sim/runs_billing.py (block, measure), which also writes results/runs/billing_sensitivity.md (the same rows to 2 decimals). order = a replica is billed from the order on, so each start pays its whole lead, beta = c x d with d the one-minute slots the engine executes (8 at the instance tier; 2 at the 72B replica tier, whose 1.5 min lead runs as 2 slots); the planners re-solve and both baseline tunings are redone at that price (billing/<case>_<tier>/tune_cost from the tier's own cost tuning, tune_floor from the dispersed-slice floor tuning with the section-3 resource rule), and every row comes from billing/<case>_<tier>/eval_cost or eval_floor. main = the paper's caliber, billed from the first serving minute plus one replica-minute per start, beta = c: every row is section 27a's row of the same name (the cost-tuned rules and fixed rerun with the same configuration on the same streams in billing/<case>_<tier>/ref_main, with equal results). Billed GPU-hours per week = (gpu_hours + starts x d / 60) / weeks with d = 1 under main, so under main they exceed section 27a's GPU-hours (serving replica-minutes only) by the starts per week / 60; USD per week = (run_cost + switch_cost) / weeks, the resource cost without the unserved-request penalty.

Means over the five seeds. Billed GPU-hours and USD per week to 1 decimal; in-class attainment (whole run) to 3 decimals, and where the mean lies within 0.01 of 0.95 also to 4 with the lowest seed; vs_tap_pct = billed GPU-hours in percent of TAP's in the same case, tier and caliber, from unrounded means, to 1 decimal, and vs_tap_pct_int the same to the nearest integer.

## 32. TAP across the six timing cells A-F on the laptop platform (arm64), r = 30, five seeds (10-14), with every laptop baseline row on the same streams

From scripts/sim/canonical_cells_laptop.py. TAP and the offline optimum come from the R1 jobs (outputs/validation_local/runs/tap/r1_<case>_<tier>, --policies offline tap, cells A-F), TAP's diagnostics from each job's diagnostics.csv. The baselines are every laptop run of the job's stream group whose offline rows equal the R1 job's on the same (timing cell, seed, week) keys in every column: in cells A-F the six-week rerun of section 3's resource-rule floor rules (runs/tuning_resource/<case>_<tier>/eval: reactive and velocity, floor-tuned (dispersed)) and the two runs of the cells-laptop queue behind section 7 (runs/cells_laptop/<case>_r30_d_inst_t8 or <case>_r30_d_rep: every other policy, the feedback rules cost-tuned on their own tier; the same with _learnprior: calendar_learn_prior); in cell C also the runs section 27a draws on and the billing round's main-caliber block (runs/billing/<case>_<tier>/ref_main). Cells are identified by their trigger-offset columns (SweepConfig.trigger_offset, parameters in section 5). Every source is a laptop run (platform column); the cloud rows of sections 1-3 draw other streams and are not compared here. Whole run (six weeks), means over the five seeds: GPU-hours per week to 1 decimal; in-class attainment to 3 decimals, and where the mean lies within 0.01 of 0.95 also to 4 with the lowest seed; reaches_0.95 compares the unrounded mean with 0.95.

32a. TAP and the offline optimum per cell. phase_share_min: the lowest share over the seeds of the committed slots BDINTERP-d takes in the advice (ADV) phase, 6 decimals (1 = the guard never leaves the plan); n_replans: size re-solves of the online correction; final_ratio: the size ratio in force at the end of the run (3 decimals); eps: TAP's trust parameter; laptop_baseline_rows: the rows of 32b in the cell.

32b. Every laptop baseline row that pairs, named as in section 27a (group as in 27d: planner, feedback rule, other). extra_over_tap_pct: GPU-hours per week in percent of TAP's in the same case, tier and cell, from unrounded means (1 decimal; _int to the nearest integer). A row two runs produce seed for seed in every column is listed once with both sources. Cell C: every row, TAP's and the offline optimum's included, is section 27a's row of the same name (checked, 79 rows: GPU-hours per week and attainment, unrounded). Every cell: each row of section 7 is the row of the same cell and name here and each row here outside cell C is one of section 7's (checked, 408 rows: GPU-hours per week and attainment, unrounded and as printed).

| case | tier | cells_without_a_laptop_row | cells_with_one | policies | laptop_row | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| c1 7B shared-4k | d_inst | A, B, C, D, E, F |  | every baseline | no laptop row | no laptop run of these streams besides the TAP job (r1_c1_inst/invariant.json: 0 laptop and 5 cloud mirrors) |
| c1 7B shared-4k | d_rep | A, B, C, D, E, F |  | every baseline | no laptop row | no laptop run of these streams besides the TAP job (r1_c1_rep/invariant.json: 0 laptop and 4 cloud mirrors) |
| c2 32B shared-4k | d_inst | A, B, D, E, F | C | velocity, cost-tuned (replica-tier tuning); velocity, floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection); reactive, cost-tuned (replica-tier tuning); reactive, floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection); reactive, floor-tuned (synchronous) | no laptop row | their laptop runs hold only the cells with one |
| c2 32B shared-4k | d_rep | A, B, D, E, F | C | velocity, floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection); reactive, floor-tuned (synchronous) | no laptop row | their laptop runs hold only the cells with one |
| c3 72B shared-4k | d_inst | A, B, D, E, F | C | velocity, cost-tuned (replica-tier tuning); reactive, cost-tuned (replica-tier tuning); reactive, floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection) | no laptop row | their laptop runs hold only the cells with one |
| c3 72B shared-4k | d_rep | A, B, D, E, F | C | velocity, floor-tuned (dispersed, previous selection) | no laptop row | their laptop runs hold only the cells with one |

## 33. TAP at r = 100 against r = 30 on the laptop platform (arm64): the R7 jobs against the R1 jobs of the same streams, cells A-F, five seeds (10-14)

33a. From scripts/sim/canonical_r100_laptop.py. TAP and the offline optimum of each R7 job (runs/tap/r7_<case>_<tier>) next to the R1 job of the same case and tier (the r = 30 rows are 32a's). The two jobs' run commands differ only in --p-in and --out, and their offline rows agree in every stream column (runs_check.STREAM_COLS without p_in, p_off and price_ratio_real) on every (timing cell, seed, week) key, so TAP runs on the same streams at both prices. r: the price ratio p_in mu / c of the rows. tap_gpu_h_wk_vs_r30_pct: TAP's GPU-hours per week at r = 100 in percent of its r = 30 value (unrounded means, 1 decimal); slo_in_vs_r30: the change of its unrounded mean attainment (4 decimals). Other columns as 32a.

## 34. The two simulation platforms on the base cell: laptop (arm64) against cloud (x86) rows of the same policy and configuration, c2 and c3, both tiers, cell C, r = 30, five seeds (10-14), six weeks

From scripts/sim/canonical_platform.py. The same simulator and arguments draw different arrival streams on the cloud boxes (x86) and on the laptop (arm64) (results/runs/JOBS_README.md, Platform); section 27a reports the laptop's streams, sections 1-3 the cloud's. Laptop rows: section 27a's (results/runs/paired_cell_c.csv). Cloud rows: results/sim/validation/tables/cells_long.csv at the main timetable, the rows of sections 1-3 and, at the instance tier, the reactive and velocity rows tuned at the replica tier (tuned_at d_rep, not in sections 1-3), which 27a also runs there; each is recomputed from its run's offset.csv and equals cells_long.csv (checked, 80 rows, unrounded means). The replica-tuned rows come from the first round's r30_d_inst and r30_d_inst_slo95, which predate the follow-up round's planner-kernel change, so only their feedback rows enter, after two checks: the first-round and re-run r30_d_rep agree on offline, reactive and velocity, and r30_d_inst's offline rows equal r30_d_inst_t8's (checked, 1680 rows, equal in GPU-hours, starts, every cost term, mean and peak replicas, requests, attainment and timeouts). A laptop and a cloud row pair when case, tier and policy agree and their runs' commands (laptop: the rebuilt command of a sensitivity-round or ablation original, checked against its CSV and log, or the START line of a rerun job's log; cloud: the phase file the box ran), parsed by run_sweep.py's parser, agree in everything the policy reads: sweep, workload, service, cost, seeds and policy configuration, with the tuned knobs compared only for the rule that reads them and the learned-timing prior only for calendar_learn_prior. Cloud rows of one configuration (an SLO-floor pick equal to the cost pick) are equal seed for seed and listed once (cloud_row joins their labels). Left out (34c): tap, which no cloud run has, and the floor-tuned (dispersed) rules whose resource-rule configuration (section 28b) no cloud run used; their previous selection is the cloud's configuration and pairs, and a floor-tuned (dispersed) rule whose configuration the resource rule kept pairs with the cloud's (SLO>=0.95, disp) row.

34a. Every pair. GPU-hours per week to 2 decimals and in-class attainment (mean over seeds) to 4; d_gpu_h_wk and d_slo_in are cloud minus laptop and d_gpu_pct is d_gpu_h_wk in percent of the laptop value (2 decimals), all from unrounded means. Groups: offline = the offline optimum; planners = calendar, calendar_learn_prior, calendar_learn, calendar_adapt, stale_calendar, forecast_ilp; feedback rules = every reactive and velocity variant; other = the remaining policies. cloud_tuned_at: the tier the cloud row's baselines were tuned at.
